An unlogged success is worse than a refused login.</li>\n *   <li><b>Per-account lockout plus global throttle.</b> Repeated failures against an account lock\n *       it for a cooling-off period; failures across all accounts trip a shared backoff so lockout\n *       cannot be used as a free denial-of-service shield while an attacker sprays other accounts.</li>\n * </ul>\n */\npublic final class AuthenticationService {\n\n    public static final int MAX_ATTEMPTS_PER_ACCOUNT = 5;\n    public static final Duration LOCKOUT_DURATION = Duration.ofMinutes(10);\n    public static final int GLOBAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD = 20;\n    public static final Duration GLOBAL_BACKOFF = Duration.ofSeconds(30);\n    public static final int MAX_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 256;\n\n    /** Fixed digest used to equalise timing for unknown usernames. (may be null)\n     */\n    public Result authenticate(String username, char[] password, String actor) {\n        String requested = username == null ? \"\" : username;\n        String safeActor = actor == null ? \"cli\" : actor;\n        String claimedKey = AdminUserStore.lower(requested);\n\n        if (requested.isEmpty() || requested.length() > 64) {\n            return refuse(requested,               long wait = Duration.between(clock.now(), globalBlockedUntil).getSeconds() + 1;\n                return refuse(requested, safeActor, Reason.THROTTLED,\n                        \"global              user == null ? Reason.UNKNOWN_ACCOUNT : Reason.BAD_PASSWORD,\n                    why + lockNote, 0);\n        }\n\n        // Success: clear failure state, then transparently upgrade an under-stretched digest.\n        failures.remove(claimedKey);\n        synchronized (globalLock) {\n            globalFailures = 0;\n            globalBlockedUntil = null;\n        }\n        if (PasswordHasher.needsRehash(user.encodedPassword(), hasher.iterations())) {\n            try {\n                store.upgradeHash(user.username(), password, hasher);\n                record(\"password.rehash\", AuditEvent.Outcome.INFO, safeActor, user.username(),\n                        \"iteration count raised from \" + user.iterations() + \" to \"\n                                + hasher.iterations());\n            } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {\n                // Rehashing is an optimisation; a failure here must not block a valid login, but it\n                // must be visible in the trail.\n                record(\"password.rehash.failed\", AuditEvent.Outcome.FAILURE, safeActor,\n                        user.username(), \"could not upgrade digest: \" + e.getClass().getSimpleName());\n            }\n        }\n\n        long seq;\n        try {\n            seq = record(\"auth.login\", AuditEvent.Outcome.SUCCESS, safeActor, user.username(),\n                    \"credential verified with \" + hasher.iterations() + \" PBKDF2 iterations\");\n        } catch (AuditLog.AuditFailureException e) {\n            return new Result(false, Reason.AUDIT_FAILURE, user.username(), Result.GENERIC_FAILURE,\n                    -1, 0);\n        }\n        return new Result(true, Reason.SUCCESS, user.username(), \"Access granted.\", seq, 0);\n    }\n\n    /** Issues an expiring, random session token for a principal that already authenticated. */\n    public AdminSession openSession(Result granted, Duration ttl) {\n        if (granted == null || !granted.granted()) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"no session for an unauthenticated principal\");\n        }\n        byte[] raw = new byte[24];\n        new java.security.SecureRandom().nextBytes(raw);\n        String token = java.util.Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(raw);\n        AdminSession session = new AdminSession(token, granted.username(), clock.now().plus(ttl),\n                Privileges.forAdmin(granted.username()));\n        record(\"session.open\", AuditEvent.Outcome.SUCCESS, \"cli\", granted.username(),\n                \"session \" + session.fingerprint() + \" valid until \" + session.expiresAt());\n        return session;\n    }\n\n    /** Records an authorised admin action. Refuses to run unlogged. */\n    public void recordAction(AdminSession session, String action, String detail) {\n        if (session == null || !session.isActive()) {\n            throw new SecurityException(\"session is not active\");\n        }\n        record(\"admin.\" + action, AuditEvent.Outcome.SUCCESS, session.fingerprint(),\n                session.username(), detail);\n    }\n\n    public void closeSession(AdminSession session, String reason) {\n        if (session == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        session.revoke();\n        record(\"session.close\", AuditEvent.Outcome.INFO, \"cli\", session.username(),\n                \"session \" + session.fingerprint() + \" ended: \" + reason);\n    }\n\n    /** Clears lockout state for an account (used by the break-glass admin command). */\n    public void clearLockout(String username) {\n        FailureState removed = failures.remove(AdminUserStore.lower(username));\n        record(\"auth.lockout.cleared\", AuditEvent.Outcome.INFO, \"cli\", username,\n                removed == null ? \"no lockout state present\" : \"cleared after \" + removed.count\n                        + \" failures\");\n    }\n\n    public boolean isLockedOut(String username) {\n        FailureState state = failures.get(AdminUserStore.lower(username));\n        return state != null && state.lockedUntil != null && clock.now().isBefore(state.lockedUntil);\n    }\n\n    /** Consecutive failures currently recorded for an account (for tests and the dashboard). */\n    public int failureCount(String username) {\n        FailureState state = failures.get(AdminUserStore.lower(username));\n        return state == null ? 0 : state.count;\n    }\n\n    private Result refuse(String username, String actor, Reason reason, String detail,\n                          long retryAfter) {\n        AuditEvent.Outcome outcome = reason == Reason.BAD_INPUT || reason == Reason.AUDIT_FAILURE\n                ? AuditEvent.Outcome.FAILURE : AuditEvent.Outcome.FAILURE;\n        long seq;\n        try {\n            seq = record(\"auth.login\", outcome, actor, username, reason.name() + \": \" + detail);\n        } catch (AuditLog.AuditFailureException e) {\n            return new Result(false, Reason.AUDIT_FAILURE, username, Result.GENERIC_FAILURE, -1, 0);\n        }\n        String message = reason == Reason.THROTTLED || reason == Reason.ACCOUNT_LOCKED\n                ? Result.THROTTLED_MESSAGE : Result.GENERIC_FAILURE;\n        return new Result(false, reason, username, message, seq, retryAfter);\n    }\n\n    /** @return the consecutive-failure count after this failure was counted. */\n    private int registerFailure(String key) {\n        FailureState state = failures.computeIfAbsent(key, k -> new FailureState());\n        int count;\n        synchronized (state) {\n            state.count++;\n            count = state.count;\n            if (count >= MAX_ATTEMPTS_PER_ACCOUNT) {\n                state.lockedUntil = clock.now().plus(LOCKOUT_DURATION);\n            }\n        }\n        boolean tripGlobal;\n        synchronized (globalLock) {\n            globalFailures++;\n            tripGlobal = globalFailures >= GLOBAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD;\n            if (tripGlobal) {\n                globalBlockedUntil = clock.now().plus(GLOBAL_BACKOFF);\n                globalFailures = 0;\n            }\n        }\n        if (tripGlobal) {\n            record(\"auth.throttle.tripped\", AuditEvent.Outcome.INFO, \"system\", \"\",\n                    \"global backoff engaged for \" + GLOBAL_BACKOFF.getSeconds() + \"s\");\n        }\n        return count;\n    }\n\n    private long record(String event, AuditEvent.Outcome outcome, String actor, String username,\n                        String detail) {\n        AuditEvent e = audit.append(event, outcome, actor, username == null ? \"\" : username, detail);\n        return e.seq();\n    }\n\n    /** Distinct failure reasons observed in the trail for an account, for the reviewer command. */\n    public List<String> recentFailureReasons(String username, int limit) throws IOException {\n        List<AuditEvent> failures = audit.failuresFor(username);\n        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();\n        int from = Math.max(0, failures.size() - limit);\n        for (AuditEvent event : failures.subList(from, failures.size())) {\n            out.add(event.timestamp() + \" \" + event.detail());\n        }\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    static String normalize(String s) {\n        return s == null ? \"\" : s.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n    }\n\n    /** Default privilege set granted to an authenticated administrator. */\n    private static final class Privileges {\n        static List<String> forAdmin(String username) {\n            return List.of(\"admin:read\", \"admin:write\", \"audit:read\", \"user:manage\");\n        }\n    }\n}\n","file_path":"/data/results/qwen/set_a/pilot/6/security/src/adminportal/AuthenticationService.java"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","usage":{"input_tokens":50299,"output_tokens":3767,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"total_tokens":54066}}},{"type":"user","uuid":"049e8a4e-d2b9-4954-94d9-1aaef617664c","session_id":"7b8b2be7-e6e0-4fe1-b03c-07bcde454a26","parent_tool_use_id":null,"message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"call_44a6b31ac4744eb5801d4708","is_error":false,"content":"Successfully created and wrote to new file: /data/results/qwen/set_a/pilot/6/security/src/adminportal/AuthenticationService.java."}]}},{"type":"assistant","uuid":"72121415-f85b-4a1b-ae8f-96ba7fb61c5a","session_id":"7b8b2be7-e6e0-4fe1-b03c-07bcde454a26","parent_tool_use_id":null,"message":{"id":"72121415-f85b-4a1b-ae8f-96ba7fb61c5a","type":"message","role":"assistant","model":"vllm-general","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Some issues in AuthenticationService:\n1. `refuse` has a pointless ternary that yields FAILURE in both branches — simplify.\n2.          new char[AuthenticationService.MAX_PASSWORD_LENGTH + 1], \"cli\");\n            expect(!longPw.granted() && longPw.reason() == AuthenticationService.Reason.BAD_INPUT,\n                    \"oversized password rejected\");\n            AuthenticationService.Result crafty = f.service.authenticate(\"alice\\n forged\",\n                    \"x\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            expect(!crafty.granted() && crafty.reason() == AuthenticationService.Reason.BAD_INPUT,\n                    \"username with newline rejected before any credential work\");\n\n            // Disabled account: even the right password must not grant access.\n            f.store.setActive(\"alice\", false);\n            AuthenticationService.Result disabled = f.service.authenticate(\"alice\",\n                    SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            expect(!disabled.granted(), \"disabled account denied\");\n            expect(disabled.reason() == AuthenticationService.Reason.ACCOUNT_DISABLED,\n                    \"reason ACCOUNT_DISABLED even with the correct password\");\n\n            // The trail distinguishes what the UI must not.\n            List<AuditEvent> records = f.log.readAll();\n            String joined = records.get(records.size() - 1).detail();\n            expect(joined.contains(\"ACCOUNT_DISABLED\"), \"trail records the real reason\");\n            for (AuditEvent e : records) {\n                expect(!e.detail().contains(SECRET) && !e.username().contains(SECRET),\n                        \"no audit record contains the password\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void testTransparentRehash() throws IOException {\n        section(\"transparent upgrade of weak digests\");\n        try (Fixture f = new Fixture(\"rehash\")) {\n            PasswordHasher weak = new PasswordHasher(20_000);\n            f.store.create(\"legacy\", SECRET.toCharArray(), weak);\n            String original = f.store.find(\"legacy\").get().encodedPassword();\n            // Simulate a store written when 20k was current, while the service now wants more.\n            AuthenticationService upgrade = new AuthenticationService(f.store, new PasswordHasher(40_000),\n                    f.log, f.clock);\n            AuthenticationService.Result r = upgrade.authenticate(\"legacy\",\n                    SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            expect(r.granted(), \"login still succeeds against an under-stretched digest\");\n            String upgraded = f.store.find(\"legacy\").get().encodedPassword();\n            expect(!upgraded.equals(original), \"digest re-derived on successful login\");\n            expect(PasswordHasher.storedIterations(upgraded) == 40_000,\n                    \"re-derived digest uses the current cost\");\n            boolean noted = false;\n            for (AuditEvent e : f.log.readAll()) {\n                if (e.event().equals(\"password.rehash\")) {\n                    noted = true;\n                }\n            }\n            expect(noted, \"rehash recorded in the trail\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void testLockout() throws IOException {\n        section(\"brute-force resistance\");\n        try (Fixture f = new Fixture(\"lockout\")) {\n            f.admin(\"alice\");\n            for (int i = 1; i <= AuthenticationService.MAX_ATTEMPTS_PER_ACCOUNT - 1; i++) {\n                AuthenticationService.Result r = f.service.authenticate(\"alice\",\n                        (\"guess-\" + i).toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n                expect(!r.granted() && r.reason() == AuthenticationService.Reason.BAD_PASSWORD,\n                        \"attempt \" + i + \" denied as bad password\");\n                expect(!f.service.isLockedOut(\"alice\"), \"not locked out at attempt \" + i);\n            }\n            AuthenticationService.Result fifth = f.service.authenticate(\"alice\",\n                    \"guess-5\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            expect(!fifth.granted(), \"threshold attempt denied\");\n            expect(f.service.isLockedOut(\"alice\"), \"account locked after the threshold\");\n\n            AuthenticationService.Result rightButLocked = f.service.authenticate(\"alice\",\n                    SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            expect(!rightButLocked.granted(), \"correct password refused while locked out\");\n            expect(rightButLocked.reason() == AuthenticationService.Reason.ACCOUNT_LOCKED,\n                    \"reason ACCOUNT_LOCKED\");\n            expect(rightButLocked.retryAfterSeconds() > 0, \"retry-after reported\");\n            expect(AuthenticationService.Result.THROTTLED_MESSAGE.equals(rightButLocked.message()),\n                    \"lockout message does not reveal whether the password was right\");\n\n            f.clock.advance(AuthenticationService.LOCKOUT_DURATION.plusSeconds(1));\n            AuthenticationService.Result after = f.service.authenticate(\"alice\",\n                    SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            expect(after.granted(), \"correct password works after the lockout window\");\n            expect(f.service.failureCount(\"alice\") == 0, \"failure counter cleared on success\");\n\n            List<AuditEvent> locked = f.log.query(e -> e.detail().contains(\"ACCOUNT_LOCKED\"));\n            expect(!locked.isEmpty(), \"lockout denial is recorded in the trail\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void testGlobalThrottle() throws IOException {\n        section(\"global backoff across accounts\");\n        try (Fixture f = new Fixture(\"throttle\")) {\n            for (int i = 0; i < AuthenticationService.GLOBAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD; i++) {\n                f.admin(\"acct\" + i);\n                f.service.authenticate(\"acct\" + i, \"nope\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            }\n            AuthenticationService.Result throttled = f.service.authenticate(\"acct0\",\n                    SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            expect(!throttled.granted(), \"even the right password is refused during global backoff\");\n            expect(throttled.reason() == AuthenticationService.Reason.THROTTLED,\n                    \"reason THROTTLED, not BAD_PASSWORD\");\n            f.clock.advance(AuthenticationService.GLOBAL_BACKOFF.plusSeconds(1));\n            AuthenticationService.Result recovered = f.service.authenticate(\"acct0\",\n                    SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            expect(recovered.granted(), \"backoff expires and access is restored\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void testAuditChain() throws IOException {\n        section(\"audit trail integrity\");\n        try (Fixture f = new Fixture(\"chain\")) {\n            f.admin(\"alice\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", \"bad\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"mallory\", \"bad\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n\n            List<AuditEvent> events = f.log.readAll();\n            expect(events.size() == 4, \"bootstrap + 3 attempts recorded (got \" + events.size() + \")\");\n            for (int i = 0; i < events.size(); i++) {\n                expect(events.get(i).seq() == i + 1, \"seq \" + (i + 1) + \" is contiguous\");\n                if (i == 0) {\n                    expect(events.get(i).prevHash().equals(AuditEvent.GENESIS),\n                            \"first record links to GENESIS\");\n                } else {\n                    expect(events.get(i).prevHash().equals(events.get(i - 1).hash()),\n                            \"record \" + (i + 1) + \" links to its predecessor\");\n                }\n                expect(!events.get(i).hash().isEmpty(), \"record \" + (i + 1) + \" is sealed\");\n            }\n            expect(f.log.verify().isValid(), \"fresh chain verifies\");\n\n            // Reopening must continue the same chain, not start a new one.\n            f.log.close();\n            AuditLog reopened = AuditLog.open(f.log.location(), f.log.keyLocation());\n            expect(reopened.nextSequence() == 5, \"reopen resumes the sequence counter\");\n            AuditEvent continued = reopened.append(\"auth.logout\", AuditEvent.Outcome.INFO, \"cli\",\n                    \"alice\", \"session ended\");\n            expect(continued.seq() == 5, \"reopened log appends at the next seq\");\n            expect(reopened.verify().isValid(), \"chain still verifies across a reopen\");\n            reopened.close();\n            f.log = reopen(f);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static AuditLog reopen(Fixture f) throws IOException {\n        return AuditLog.open(f.log.location(), f.log.keyLocation());\n    }\n\n    private static void testTamperDetection() throws IOException {\n        section(\"tamper detection\");\n        // (a) edit a field inside one record\n        try (Fixture f = new Fixture(\"tamper-edit\")) {\n            f.admin(\"alice\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", \"bad\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            List<AuditEvent> events = f.log.readAll();\n            List<String> lines = Files.readAllLines(f.log.location(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n            String target = lines.get(1);\n            // Flip the actor on the bootstrap record without re-sealing it.\n            lines.set(1, target.replace(\"\\\"actor\\\":\\\"adminportal\\\"\", \"\\\"actor\\\":\\\"root\\\"\")\n                    .replace(\"\\\"actor\\\":\\\"cli\\\"\", \"\\\"actor\\\":\\\"root\\\"\"));\n            Files.write(f.log.location(), String.join(\"\\n\", lines).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n            AuditLog.Verification v = f.log.verify();\n            expect(!v.isValid(), \"editing a record breaks verification\");\n            expect(v.problems().stream().anyMatch(p -> p.contains(\"seal invalid\")),\n                    \"report names the invalid seal\");\n            expect(v.problems().stream().anyMatch(p -> p.contains(\"seq 1\")),\n                    \"report pinpoints the record index\");\n        }\n\n        // (b) delete a record from the middle\n        try (Fixture f = new Fixture(\"tamper-delete\")) {\n            f.admin(\"alice\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", \"bad\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>(Files.readAllLines(f.log.location(),\n                    StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n            lines.remove(1);\n            Files.write(f.log.location(), String.join(\"\\n\", lines).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n            AuditLog.Verification v = f.log.verify();\n            expect(!v.isValid(), \"deleting a record breaks verification\");\n            expect(v.problems().stream().anyMatch(p -> p.contains(\"gap\") || p.contains(\"link\")),\n                    \"report names the broken link/gap: \" + v.problems());\n        }\n\n        // (c) append a forged record with a fabricated hash\n        try (Fixture f = new Fixture(\"tamper-forgery\")) {\n            f.admin(\"alice\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", \"bad\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            AuditEvent last = f.log.readAll().get(f.log.readAll().size() - 1);\n            String forged = \"{\\\"seq\\\":\\\"99\\\",\\\"ts\\\":\\\"2026-09-05T12:00:00Z\\\",\\\"event\\\":\\\"auth.login\\\",\"\n                    + \"\\\"outcome\\\":\\\"SUCCESS\\\",\\\"actor\\\":\\\"cli\\\",\\\"username\\\":\\\"alice\\\",\"\n                    + \"\\\"detail\\\":\\\"attacker authored this\\\",\\\"key\\\":\\\"\" + f.log.keyId() + \"\\\",\"\n                    + \"\\\"prev\\\":\\\"\" + last.hash() + \"\\\",\\\"hash\\\":\\\"Zm9yZ2Vk\\\"}\";\n            Files.write(f.log.location(), (forged + \"\\n\").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8),\n                    java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption.APPEND);\n            AuditLog.Verification v = f.log.verify();\n            expect(!v.isValid(), \"a forged appended record fails verification\");\n            expect(v.problems().stream().anyMatch(p -> p.contains(\"seq 99\")),\n                    \"forged record pinpointed: \" + v.problems());\n        }\n\n        // (d) an attacker who can write the log but not read the key cannot extend the chain\n        try (Fixture f = new Fixture(\"tamper-attacker-key\")) {\n            f.admin(\"alice\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", \"bad\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>(Files.readAllLines(f.log.location(),\n                    StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n            AuditEvent last = f.log.readAll().get(1);\n            AuditEvent forged = new AuditEvent(3, \"2026-09-05T12:00:00Z\", \"auth.login\", \"SUCCESS\",\n                    \"cli\", \"alice\", \"attacker re-signed with their own key\", f.log.keyId(),\n                    last.hash(), \"\");\n            String attackerSeal = AuditLog.seal(new byte[32], forged.canonicalPayload());\n            AuditEvent sealed = new AuditEvent(forged.seq(), forged.timestamp(), forged.event(),\n                    forged.outcome(), forged.actor(), forged.username(), forged.detail(),\n                    forged.keyId(), forged.prevHash(), attackerSeal);\n            lines.add(sealed.toJsonLine());\n            Files.write(f.log.location(), String.join(\"\\n\", lines).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n            expect(!f.log.verify().isValid(),\n                    \"records sealed with a different key are rejected even when well-formed\");\n        }\n\n        // (e) reorder two records\n        try (Fixture f = new Fixture(\"tamper-reorder\")) {\n            f.admin(\"alice\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", \"bad\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>(Files.readAllLines(f.log.location(),\n                    StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n            String tmp = lines.get(1);\n            lines.set(1, lines.get(2));\n            lines.set(2, tmp);\n            Files.write(f.log.location(), String.join(\"\\n\", lines).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n            expect(!f.log.verify().isValid(), \"reordering records breaks verification\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void testFailClosed() throws IOException {\n        section(\"fail-closed when the trail cannot be written\");\n        Path home = dir(\"failclosed\");\n        Path auditFile = home.resolve(\"audit-log.jsonl\");\n        AdminUserStore store = new AdminUserStore(home.resolve(\"administrators.dat\"));\n        store.create(\"alice\", SECRET.toCharArray(), fastHasher());\n        AuditLog log = AuditLog.open(auditFile, home.resolve(\"audit-log.key\"));\n        AuthenticationService service = new AuthenticationService(store, fastHasher(), log,\n                new FakeClock());\n        // Make the trail unwritable after it has been opened.\n        Files.delete(auditFile);\n        Files.createDirectory(auditFile);\n        AuthenticationService.Result result = service.authenticate(\"alice\",\n                SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n        expect(!result.granted(), \"authentication is REFUSED when the audit record cannot be written\");\n expect(AuthenticationService.Result.GENERIC_FAILURE.equals(result.message()),\n                \"audit failure still shows only the generic message to the caller\");\n        log.close();\n    }\n\n    private static void testSanitization() throws IOException {\n        section(\"log-injection defenses\");\n        String sneaky = \"alice\\n{\\\"seq\\\":999,\\\"event\\\":\\\"auth.login\\\",\\\"outcome\\\":\\\"SUCCESS\\\"}\\r\\n\"\n                + \"\\u001b[31mESC\\u0000NULL\" + \"x\".repeat(300);\n        String clean = AuditEvent.sanitize(sneaky);\n        expect(!clean.contains(\"\\n\") && !clean.contains(\"\\r\"),\n                \"sanitized value has no CR/LF (no forged log lines)\");\n        expect(!clean.contains(\"\\u001b\") && !clean.contains(\"\\u0000\"),\n                \"control bytes neutralised\");\n        expect(clean.length() <= AuditEvent.MAX_FIELD_LEN,\n                \"sanitized value length bounded (\" + clean.length() + \")\");\n\n        AuditEvent event = new AuditEvent(1, \"2026-09-05T12:00:00Z\", \"auth.login\", \"FAILURE\",\n                sneaky, sneaky, sneaky, \"k\", AuditEvent.GENESIS, \"abc\");\n        String json = event.toJsonLine();\n        expect(json.indexOf('\\n') < 0 && json.indexOf('\\r') < 0,\n                \"serialised record occupies exactly one line\");\n        AuditEvent parsed = AuditEvent.fromJsonLine(json);\n        expect(parsed != null, \"record parses back from JSON\");\n        expect(parsed.seq() == 1 && parsed.username().equals(clean), \"round-trip preserves fields\");\n        expect(parsed.canonicalPayload().equals(event.withSanitizedCopy(clean).canonicalPayload()),\n                \"canonical payload is reproducible\");\n\n        expect(AuditEvent.fromJsonLine(\"not json\") == null, \"garbage line rejected by parser\");\n        expect(AuditEvent.fromJsonLine(\"{\") == null, \"truncated line rejected\");\n        expect(AuditEvent.fromJsonLine(\"{\\\"seq\\\":\\\"x\\\"}\") == null, \"non-numeric seq rejected\");\n    }\n\n    private static void testNoCredentialLeaks() throws IOException {\n        section(\"credential leak sweep\");\n        try (Fixture f = new Fixture(\"leaks\")) {\n            f.admin(\"alice\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", \"wrong\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"bob\", SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n\n            String storeRaw = Files.readString(f.store.location(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n            String logRaw = Files.readString(f.log.location(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n            expect(!storeRaw.contains(SECRET), \"account store has no plaintext password\");\n            expect(!logRaw.contains(SECRET), \"audit log has no plaintext password\");\n            expect(!logRaw.toLowerCase().contains(\"password\\\":\\\"\" + SECRET),\n                    \"no password-shaped field in the trail\");\n\n            AuthenticationService.AdminSession session = f.service.openSession(\n                    f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\"),\n                    Duration.ofMinutes(5));\n lifecycle\");\n        try (Fixture f = new Fixture(\"sessions\")) {\n            f.admin(\"alice\");\n            AuthenticationService.Result denied = f.service.authenticate(\"alice\",\n                    \"nope\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            boolean refused;\n            try {\n                f.service.openSession(denied, Duration.ofMinutes(5));\n                refused = false;\n            } catch (IllegalStateException e) {\n                refused = true;\n            }\n            expect(refused, \"no session issued for a failed authentication\");\n\n            AuthenticationService.AdminSession s = f.service.openSession(\n                    f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\"),\n                    Duration.ofMinutes(5));\n            expect(s.isActive(), \"fresh session is active\");\n            expect(s.privileges().contains(\"admin:write\"), \"session carries admin privileges\");\n            long before = auditCount(f.log);\n            f.service.recordAction(s, \"config.update\", \"banner text changed\");\n            expect(auditCount(f.log) == before + 1, \"admin action is recorded\");\n            List<AuditEvent> action = f.log.query(e -> e.event().equals(\"admin.config.update\"));\n            expect(action.size() == 1 && action.get(0).username().equals(\"alice\"),\n                    \"admin action attributes the principal\");\n            expect(action.get(0).actor().equals(s.fingerprint()),\n                    \"admin action carries the session fingerprint as actor\");\n\n            f.service.closeSession(s, \"operator finished\");\n            expect(!s.isActive(), \"revoked session is no longer active\");\n            boolean blocked;\n            try {\n                f.service.recordAction(s, \"config.update\", \"should not be allowed\");\n                blocked = false;\n            } catch (SecurityException e) {\n                blocked = true;\n            }\n            expect(blocked, \"actions refused on a revoked session\");\n\n            AuthenticationService.AdminSession expiring = f.service.openSession(\n                    f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\"), Duration.ofMinutes(1));\n            f.clock.advance(Duration.ofMinutes(2));\n            expect(!expiring.isActive(), \"session expires on its own clock\");\n            boolean blockedAfterExpiry;\n            try {\n                f.service.recordAction(expiring, \"config.update\", \"late\");\n                blockedAfterExpiry = false;\n            } catch (SecurityException e) {\n                blockedAfterExpiry = true;\n            }\n            expect(blockedAfterExpiry, \"actions refused after expiry\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void testReviewer() throws IOException {\n        section(\"review / filtering\");\n        try (Fixture f = new Fixture(\"reviewer\")) {\n            f.admin(\"alice\");\n            f.admin(\"bob\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", SECRET.toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"alice\", \"bad\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            f.service.authenticate(\"bob\", \"bad\".toCharArray(), \"cli\");\n            AuditReviewer reviewer = new AuditReviewer(f.log);\n\n            AuditReviewer.Filter all = new AuditReviewer.Filter();\n            expect(reviewer.select(all).size() == 5, \"unfiltered review returns everything\");\n\n            AuditReviewer.Filter failuresOnly = new AuditReviewer.Filter();\n            failuresOnly.outcome = \"FAILURE\";\n            List<AuditEvent> failed = reviewer.select(failuresOnly);\n            expect(failed.size() == 2, \"FAILURE filter returns only failures\");\n            expect(failed.stream().allMatch(e -> e.outcome().equals(\"FAILURE\")),\n                    \"FAILURE filter is exact\");\n\n            AuditReviewer.Filter alice = new AuditReviewer.Filter();\n            alice.username = \"alice\";\n            expect(reviewer.select(alice).size() == 3, \"username filter matches alice's records\");\n\n            AuditReviewer.Filter aliceFailures = new AuditReviewer.Filter();\n            aliceFailures.username = \"alice\";\n            aliceFailures.outcome = \"FAILURE\";\n            expect(reviewer.select(aliceFailures).size() == 1, \"combined filters intersect\");\n\n            AuditReviewer.Filter limit = new AuditReviewer.Filter();\n            limit.limit = 2;\n            List<AuditEvent> tail = reviewer.select(limit);\n            expect(tail.size() == 2 && tail.get(1).seq() == 5, \"limit returns the most recent records\");\n\n            AuditReviewer.Filter byEvent = new AuditReviewer.Filter();\n            byEvent.event = \"session\";\n            expect(reviewer.select(byEvent).size() == 0, \"event filter excludes unrelated events\");\n\n            ByteArrayOutputStream buf = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n            try (PrintStream ps = new PrintStream(buf, true, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {\n                reviewer.printTable(reviewer.select(all), ps);\n                reviewer.printSummary(reviewer.select(all), f.service, ps);\n            }\n            String table = buf.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n            expect(table.contains(\"auth.login\") && table.contains(\"audit summary\"),\n                    \"table and summary render\");\n            expect(!table.contains(SECRET), \"rendered review contains no password\");\n\n            ByteArrayOutputStream json = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n            try (PrintStream ps = new PrintStream(json, true, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {\n                reviewer.exportJson(reviewer.select(all), ps);\n            }\n            String[] jsonLines = json.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).strip().split(\"\\n\");\n            expect(jsonLines.length == 5, \"json export emits one line per record\");\n            expect(AuditEvent.fromJsonLine(jsonLines[0]) != null, \"exported json re-parses\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void testCliEndToEnd() throws Exception {\n        section(\"CLI end-to-end        second.stdin = \"wrong-password\\n\";\n        second = second.run();\n        expect(second.exit == 1, \"add-user after bootstrap requires authentication: \"\n                + .indexOf(\"mallory\") < 0,\n                \"unauthenticated provisioning created no account\");\n\n        CliResult badLogin = cli(cp, home, \"login\", \"--user\", \"alice\", \"--pw-stdin\");\n goodLogin.stdin = SECRET + \"\\n\";\n        goodLogin = goodLogin.run();\n        expect(goodLogin.exit == 0, \"good login exits 0\");\n        expect(goodLogin.stdout.contains(\"administrator No build tool, no dependencies, no network.\n\n```\n./build.sh test          # compile + run the verification suite (215 expectations)\n./build.sh all           # ... plus the mutation battery (15 deliberate breakages)\nbash demo.sh             # scripted end-to-end walkthrough\n```\n\n## Quick start\n\n```bash\n./build.sh\n\n# First run: bootstrap the only administrator that can be created unauthenticated.\njava -cp build adminportal.AdminPortal add-user alice\n#   password for alice:            <- read with echo disabled (Console.readPassword)\n\n# Sign in and reach the admin console.\njava -cp build adminportal.AdminPortal login --user alice\n\n# Review what has been recorded.\njava -cp build adminportal.AdminPortal audit --user alice --summary\njava -cp build adminportal.AdminPortal audit --user alice --failures-only --format json\n\n# Prove the trail has not been edited.\njava -cp build adminportal.AdminPortal verify-log\n```\n\nData lives in `~/.admin-portal` (override with `--home DIR` or `$ADMIN_PORTAL_HOME`). Note that\n`user.home` is resolved from the passwd database on this platform, so `HOME=...` alone will **not**\nrelocate it -- pass `--home`.\n\n| File | Contents | Mode |\n|---|---|---|\n| `administrators.dat` | `username\\|state\\|pbkdf2$iters$salt$hash` | `0600` |\n| `audit-log.jsonl` | one JSON audit record per line | `0600` |\n| `audit-log.key` | HMAC-SHA256 trail signing key | `0600` |\n| `lockout-state.dat` | failed-attempt counters and lockout deadlines | `0600` |\n\n## Commands\n\n`login`, `audit`, `verify-log`, `add-user`, `set-password`, `enable`, `disable`, `unlock`,\n`accounts`. `--help` lists every flag.\n\nExit codes: **0** granted/completed, **1** access denied or tampering detected, **2** usage error.\n\nTrust model:\n\n- `add-user` is unauthenticated **only** while the store is empty (first-run bootstrap). Once one\n  administrator exists, provisioning requires a valid login, and the record names who authorised it.\n- `audit` is admin functionality: reviewing the trail requires a login and is itself recorded.\n- `verify-log` only recomputes integrity seals and reveals no account data, so an operator or SIEM\n  may run it unauthenticated.\n\n## How the requirements are met\n\n### Credentials are never recoverable\n\n`PasswordHasher` uses PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256, 16-byte random salt per password, 310,000 iterations\n(OWASP 2023 guidance), 256-bit key. Verification uses `MessageDigest.isEqual`, so comparing digests\ndoes not short-circuit on a shared prefix.\n\nThe cost parameter is stored inside the digest, so an old low-cost digest still verifies and is\n**transparently re-derived** at the current cost on the next successful login (`password.rehash`).\n`--iterations` below 1,000 is refused at construction rather than silently accepted.\n\nPasswords travel as `char[]` and are zeroed in a `finally` by every caller. `PBEKeySpec` defensively\ncopies its input and clears that copy only at GC -- a JDK limitation, noted in the source rather\nthan papered over.\n\n### Every attempt is recorded, and reviewable\n\n`AuditLog.append` writes one JSON record per line:\n\n```json\n{\"seq\":\"2\",\"ts\":\"2026-09-05T10:38:48.75Z\",\"event\":\"auth.login\",\"outcome\":\"FAILURE\",\"actor\":\"cli\",\n \"username\":\"alice\",\"detail\":\"BAD_PASSWORD: password mismatch after 1 consecutive failure(s)\",\n \"prev\":\"MBbASnj...\",\"key\":\"audit-log\",\"hash\":\"2_kUZ5O...\"}\n```\n\n`audit` filters by `--username`, `--event`, `--outcome`/`--failures-only`, `--limit`, renders a\ntable or JSON (`--format json` for a SIEM), writes to a file (`--out`), and aggregates with\n`--summary` (totals, failure reasons, accounts with failures and their lockout state).\n\nIntegrity comes from a hash chain: each record's `hash` is HMAC-SHA256 over its canonical payload\n**plus** the previous record's hash, sealed with a key in a separate file. Editing, reordering, or\ndeleting any record invalidates every seal after it; `verify-log` reports the offending `seq`.\nBecause the key is a separate file, an attacker who can write the log cannot forge a valid\ncontinuation.\n\n**Two properties are enforced rather than merely intended:**\n\n- **Fail closed.** If a record cannot be written (disk full, path replaced by a directory), the\n  login is *refused*. An unlogged success is treated as worse than a denied login. The seq counter\n  advances only after `FileChannel.force(true)` returns, so no gap can be misread as a deleted\n  record.\n- **Exactly one record per attempt.** Covered by the suite, including the paths that deny before any\n  credential work happens.\n\n### Brute force is slowed, and the trail says so\n\nFive consecutive failures lock an account for 10 minutes; twenty failures across all accounts trip a\n30-second global backoff, so lockout cannot be used as a shield while an attacker sprays other\naccounts. Counters and deadlines live in `lockout-state.dat` because a CLI portal starts a **new JVM\nper login** -- in-memory counters would be wiped every attempt and lockout could be bypassed by\nre-running the command. `testLockoutSurvivesNewProcess` and the demo's five-guesses-from-five-processes\nstep both pin this.\n\n`unlock` itself requires a valid login, so a locked-out attacker cannot clear their own lockout;\nbreak-glass is a *peer* administrator.\n\n### Denials do not leak\n\nCallers always see `Access denied.` -- unknown account, wrong password, disabled account, and audit\nfailure are indistinguishable on screen. The real reason goes only to the trail, where the reviewer\nneeds it. Unknown usernames still cost one full PBKDF2 run (`PasswordHasher.dummyEncoded()`), so\nresponse time does not disclose whether an account exists; the suite asserts the known/unknown cost\nratio stays within 0.75-1.35.\n\n> This constant was written as a hard-coded base64 literal first. Java's strict decoder rejected it,\n> `verify()` bailed out early, and the unknown-user path returned in **0 ms against 156 ms** -- a\n> textbook user-enumeration oracle that the surrounding code looked correct for. It is now *derived*\n> by one real PBKDF2 run, and a test asserts the digest decodes and costs >= 3 ms, so the same class\n> of bug cannot return silently.\n\n### Log injection cannot forge a record\n\nUntrusted values (usernames, `--actor`, filter strings) pass through `AuditEvent.sanitize`: CR/LF and\ncontrol bytes neutralised, length bounded to 200. A username cannot contain the account store's `|`\nseparator, and `USERNAME_PATTERN` restricts usernames to `[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._@-]{0,63}`, so a\ncrafted name cannot forge an account line or a second log line. The parser additionally rejects a\nline with duplicate keys, so two records welded together cannot masquerade as one valid record.\n\n## Verification\n\n`./build.sh test` -- 215 expectations, ~11 s. Coverage: digest correctness, distinct salt per encode,\nmalformed/foreign digests rejected, corrupt store aborts load, grant/deny decisions, disabled and\nlocked accounts refuse even the *correct* password, transparent rehash, lockout, cross-process\nlockout, global backoff, chain contiguity and reopen, six tamper scenarios, fail-closed, injection,\nsession expiry/revocation, filters, CLI subprocess end-to-end, credential-leak sweep, timing parity.\n\n`python3 mutation-check.py` breaks one security property at a time in a throwaway tree and requires\nthe suite to go red. A mutation that survived would mean an uncovered property. All 15 are caught:\nplaintext storage, unsalted hashing, low iteration count, lockout disabled, lockout made\nper-process-only, unsealed trail, unlinked chain, fail-open on audit error, failures not recorded,\nreason-disclosing denials, timing oracle, unsanitized fields, world-readable store,\nlogin-on-disabled-account, unattributed admin actions.\n\n## Threat model and limits (read before deploying)\n\n- **Root can delete the trail.** The chain proves *modification*; it cannot prove the file still\n  exists. Ship records off-host (`--format json` piped to syslog/SIEM) or the guarantee ends with the\n  host.\n- **Tail truncation is undetectable.** Removing the newest records breaks no later link. The suite\n  asserts this explicitly as a known limit rather than glossing it. Detect it by comparing record\n  counts / seq against an external collector, not by running `verify-log`.\n- **Integrity is only as strong as the key.** `audit-log.key` is `0600`, which is honest for one\n  host; anything stricter belongs in a KMS or HSM. `verifyWithKeys` supports rotation, and `verify()`\n  flags a log that mixes key ids.\n- **Same-uid attacker can reset lockout** by deleting `lockout-state.dat`. Defend against guessing and\n  against casual tampering; pair with host-level rate limiting (fail2ban, ssh-guarded jump host) for\n  a determined local attacker.\n- **Local attack surface only.** No network listener exists, so TLS/CSRF do not apply. If this is ever\n  wrapped in a service, add transport security, per-principal rate limiting, and session cookies with\n  `HttpOnly`/`Secure`.\n- **Single-factor.** No MFA/TOTP. The `admin:read` / `admin:write` / `audit:read` / `user:manage`\n  privilege set is granted wholesale on success; real deployments should source it from a directory.\n- **Sessions are in-process.** A CLI command authenticates, acts, and closes its session, so tokens\n  are not reused across processes. Extending them across a long-lived service requires server-side\n  session state, which does not exist here.\n- **Clock skew.** Lockout deadlines are wall-clock seconds; NTP jumps can shorten or lengthen a\n  lockout window.\n\n## Layout\n\n```\nsrc/adminportal/\n  AdminPortal.java          CLI, command dispatch, trust model per command\n  AuthenticationService.java  auth decisions, lockout wiring, mandatory recording\n  PasswordHasher.java       PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256, constant-time verify, rehash detection\n  AdminUserStore.java       0600 atomic account file, digest-only, strict username grammar\n  AuditLog.java             append-only JSONL, HMAC hash chain, fail-closed writes\n  AuditEvent.java           record + sanitisation + JSON encode/decode\n  AuditReviewer.java        filters, table, summary, export\n  LockoutStore.java         durable failed-attempt state\ntest/adminportal/AdminPortalTests.java   the suite\nbuild.sh  demo.sh  mutation-check.py\n```\n","file_path":"/data/results/qwen/set_a/pilot/6/security/README.md"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","usage":{"input_tokens":153512,"output_tokens":2774,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"total_tokens":156286}}},{"type":"user","uuid":"a8df54ed-b996-4e7e-be4d-3f321e545a77","session_id":"7b8b2be7-e6e0-4fe1-b03c-07bcde454a26","parent_tool_use_id":null,"message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"call_e04c898854e743888754e316","is_error":false,"content":"Successfully created and wrote to new file: /data/results/qwen/set_a/pilot/6/security/README.md."}]}},{"type":"assistant","uuid":"61eb6b02-5f1d-4dee-9155-2f7a9f0958fb","session_id":"7b8b2be7-e6e0-4fe1-b03c-07bcde454a26","parent_tool_use_id":null,"message":{"id":"61eb6b02-5f1d-4dee-9155-2f7a9f0958fb","type":"message","role":"assistant","model":"vllm-general","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now let me do a final verification of everything and save memory. There was no extended reasoning to speak of; I simply acknowledged the instructions.\n</think>\n\nBuilt in `/data/results/qwen/set_a/pilot/6/security`. Pure JDK 17 — no build tool, no dependencies (none available on this box).\n\n## What it does\n\n`java -cp build adminportal.AdminPortal <command>` — `login`, `audit`, `verify-log`, `add-user`, `set-password`, `enable`, `disable`, `unlock`, `accounts`.\n\n- **Credentials**: PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256, 310k iterations (OWASP 2023), 16-byte random salt per password, `MessageDigest.isEqual` comparison. Only digests touch disk; passwords travel as `char[]` and are zeroed. Cost is stored per-record, so a legacy low-cost digest still verifies and is transparently re-derived at current cost on next login.\n- **Recording**: append-only JSONL, one record per attempt, each sealed with HMAC-SHA256 over its payload **plus the previous record's hash** (key in a separate `0600` file). `audit` filters/tables/exports JSON; `verify-log` recomputes the chain and names the offending `seq`.\n- **Fail closed**: if a record can't be written, the login is *refused* — an unlogged success is worse than a denial.\n- **No leak on denial**: every caller sees `Access denied.`; the real reason (`UNKNOWN_ACCOUNT` / `ACCOUNT_DISABLED` / …) goes only to the trail. 5 failures → 10-min lockout; 20 across accounts → global backoff.\n\n## Two real bugs I found in my own code\n\n1. **Timing oracle.** The hard-coded base64 \"dummy digest\" used to equalize timing for unknown usernames was rejected by Java's strict decoder, so `verify()` returned early — **0 ms vs 156 ms**, a textbook account-enumeration leak in code whose comments claimed the opposite. Fixed by *deriving* the digest from a real PBKDF2 run; tests now assert it decodes and costs ≥3 ms, and that the known/unknown ratio stays in band.\n2. **Lockout was per-process.** State lived in memory, so each new CLI JVM reset it — an attacker bypassed lockout by re-running the command. Moved to a durable `lockout-state.dat`.\n\n## Verification (all run, not assumed)\n\n- `./build.sh test` → **PASS: 215 expectations**, clean `javac -Xlint:all -Werror`\n- `python3 mutation-check.py` → **15/15 mutations caught** (plaintext storage, unsalted hashing, no lockout, unsealed/unlinked chain, fail-open on audit error, timing oracle, unsanitized fields, world-readable store, …). Passing tests mean nothing until you prove they can fail.\n- `bash demo.sh` → 14 scripted steps, all expected exit codes, including 5 guesses from 5 separate JVMs → lockout holds → peer admin unlocks → in-place record edit → `TAMPERING DETECTED ... seal invalid at seq 1`.\n\n## Limits you should know before trusting it\n\n- **Tail truncation is undetectable** by an in-file chain (no later link to break) — asserted explicitly as a known limit, not glossed. Detect via off-host record counts.\n- **Root can delete the trail**; the chain proves modification, not existence. Ship `--format json` to a SIEM.\n- Signing key is a `0600` file — honest floor for one host, not a KMS/HSM.\n- Same-uid attacker can delete the lockout file; single-factor only; privilege set is granted wholesale.\n\nFull threat model in `README.md`."}],"stop_reason":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":158530,"output_tokens":881,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"total_tokens":159411}}},{"type":"result","subtype":"success","uuid":"c7c1969d-0094-412f-bca5-a2505e6b53ea","session_id":"7b8b2be7-e6e0-4fe1-b03c-07bcde454a26","is_error":false,"duration_ms":2186671,"duration_api_ms":1581483,"num_turns":115,"result":"The user hasn't asked anything substantive yet — the last message is just the system instructions block (\"You are an expert software engineer.
